# Training Core Database  
# 2,967 exercise sets across 8 training phases (January - July 2026)  
# Post-bilateral total shoulder replacement training program

## Column Definitions  
- PhaseID: Training phase (1-8)  
- PhaseWeek: Week number within the phase  
- ProgramDay: Day within the training week (1-4)  
- SessionDate: Date of the training session  
- WorkoutName: Name/type of the workout  
- ExerciseName: Exercise performed  
- Category: Pull, Push, Legs, Arms_Biceps, Arms_Triceps, Core, Carry, Cardio, Warmup  
- SetNumber: Set number within the exercise  
- WeightLbs: Total bilateral weight in pounds  
- Reps: Repetitions performed  
- DurationSeconds: Duration for timed exercises  
- RIR: Reps in Reserve (proximity to failure)  
- Notes: Session and exercise notes
